// Our names for the functions and globals of 1.0 (NA 1.0, ROM SHA-1 47ae795a...), one per line: name = address; // where the name comes from.
// See docs/reference-rig.md#the-symbol-map-roadmap-08 for the naming rules.

gspF3DEX2_fifoTextStart = 0x80001360; // library: the F3DEX2 fifo 2.08 microcode's text (the gfx task's ucode, size 0x1000): the boot DMAs its first 0xF80 bytes to IMEM 0x1080, and the overlays follow at +0xF80 (the end-of-task one), +0x1018 and +0x1188 (the lighting one)
Rsp.F3dex2DoneOverlay = 0x800022e0; // coined; docs/systems/rendering.md: F3DEX2's overlay at ucode +0xF80, 0x98 bytes loaded to IMEM 0x1000 when the display list ends (it halts with break)
Rsp.F3dex2LightingOverlay = 0x800024e8; // coined; docs/systems/rendering.md: F3DEX2's lighting overlay at ucode +0x1188, 0x208 bytes loaded to IMEM 0x12D8 by G_VTX when G_LIGHTING is on: it turns each light's direction by the modelview 3x3 and normalises it (vrsq) to bytes of 128 a unit, then per vertex adds the ambient and each light's colour times its dot with the vertex's raw s8 normal, clamped to 255, and jumps back to IMEM 0x1454 from its end at 0x14D8
